        load = ops.load('primals_1', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf1', get_index_1)
        sub = ops.sub(load, load_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_2)
        constant = ops.constant(27.0, torch.float32)
        truediv = ops.truediv(load_2, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1e-05, torch.float32)
        add = ops.add(truediv, constant_1)
        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add)
        mul = ops.mul(sub, rsqrt)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('primals_2', get_index_3)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(0.34412564994580647, torch.float32)

        mul_2 = ops.mul(mul, mul_1)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf4', get_index_4, mul_2, None)
        return store

class op458_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 196608, p1: 49}
    index0 = 49*p0 + p1
    index1 = p0
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf450', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf457', get_index_1)
        sigmoid = ops.sigmoid(load_1)
        mul = ops.mul(load, sigmoid)
        constant = ops.constant(2.0, torch.float32)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(mul, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0.2, torch.float32)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(mul_1, constant_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf419', get_index_2)
        add = ops.add(mul_2, load_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_3 = ops.load('buf450', get_index_3)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_4 = ops.load('buf457', get_index_4)
        sigmoid_1 = ops.sigmoid(load_4)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(load_3, sigmoid_1)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(2.0, torch.float32)
        mul_4 = ops.mul(mul_3, constant_2)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(0.2, torch.float32)
        mul_5 = ops.mul(mul_4, constant_3)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_5 = ops.load('buf419', get_index_5)
        add_1 = ops.add(mul_5, load_5)
        neg = ops.neg(add_1)
